(supabaseAdmin.from(\"profiles\") as any)\n        .select(\"id\")\n        .eq(\"email\", orgInfo.requested_by_email.toLowerCase())\n        .single();\n\n      if (userProfile) {\n        const const memberRole = roleMap[orgInfo.requested_by_role] || \"teacher\";\n\n        // Check if already a member (idempotent)\n        // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n        const { data: existing } = await (supabaseAdmin.from(\"org_memberships\") as any)\n          .select(\"id\")\n          .eq(\"org_id\", orgId)\n          .eq(\"user_id\", userProfile.id)\n          .single();\n\n        if (!existing) {\n          // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n          await (supabaseAdmin.from(\"org_memberships\") as any).insert({\n            org_id: orgId,\n            user_id: userProfile.id,\n            role: memberRole,\n          });\n          console.warn(`[APPROVE] Auto-added ${orgInfo.requested_by_email} as ${memberRole}`);\n        }\n      } else {\n        console.warn(\n          `[APPROVE] User ${orgInfo.requested_by_email} not found in profiles ÔÇö manual add needed`\n        );\n      }\n    }\n  } catch (memberErr) {\n  // ÔöÇÔöÇ Org approved email (fire-and-forget) ÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇ\n  try {\n    // eslint-disable-next-line .single();\n\n    if (org?.requested_by_email) {\n      sendEmail({\n        to: org.requested_by_email,\n        subject: `Your institution has been approved! ­ƒÅ½`,\n        html: Orgs expiring in next 30 days\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { data: expiringSoon } = await (\n    supabaseAdmin.from(\"organizations\") as any\n  )\n    .select(\"id, name, type, access_expires_at, max_students\")\n    .eq(\"status\", \"active\")\n    .not(\"access_expires_at\", \"is\", null)\n    .lte(\"access_expires_at\", in30d)\n    .gte(\"access_expires_at\", now.toISOString())\n    .order(\"access_expires_at\");\n\n  // Expired (suspended because of expiry)\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { data: expired } = await (supabaseAdmin.from(\"organizations\") as any)\n    .select(\"id, name, type, access_expires_at\")\n    .eq(\"status\", \"suspended\")\n    .not(\"access_expires_at\", \"is\", null)\n    .lt(\"access_expires_at\", now.toISOString())\n    .order(\"access_expires_at\");\n\n  // Orgs with max_students ÔÇö check capacity\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { data: withLimits } = await (\n    supabaseAdmin.from(\"organizations\") as any\n  )\n    .select(\"id, name, type, max_students\")\n    .eq(\"status\", \"active\")\n    .not(\"max_students\", \"is\", null);\n\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const nearCapacity: any[] = [];\n  for (const org of withLimits || []) {\n    const count = await getOrgStudentCount(org.id);\n    const pct = Math.round((count / org.max_students) * 100);\n    if (pct >= 80) {\n      nearCapacity.push({\n        id: org.id,\n        name: org.name,\n        type: org.type,\n        current: count,\n        max: org.max_students,\n        pct,\n      });\n    }\n  }\n  nearCapacity.sort((a, b) => b.pct - a.pct);\n\n  // Add daysLeft to expiringSoon\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const expiringWithDays = (expiringSoon || []).map((o: any) => ({\n    ...o,\n    daysLeft: Math.ceil(\n      (new Date(o.access_expires_at).getTime() - now.getTime()) / 86400000\n    ),\n  }));\n\n  return {\n    expiringSoon: expiringWithDays,\n    expired: expired || [],\n    nearCapacity,\n  };\n}\n\n// ÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇ Platform KPIs ÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇ\n\nexport async function getAdminPlatformStats() {\n  await requireSuperAdmin();\n\n  // Total users\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { count: totalUsers } = await (\n    supabaseAdmin.from(\"profiles\") as any\n  ).select(\"id\", { count: \"exact\", head: true });\n\n  // Active orgs\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { count: activeOrgs } = await (\n    supabaseAdmin.from(\"organizations\") as any\n  )\n    .select(\"id\", { count: \"exact\", head: true })\n    .eq(\"status\", \"active\");\n\n  // Plan distribution\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { data: planData } = await (\n    supabaseAdmin.from(\"profiles\") as any\n  ).select(\"plan\");\n\n  const plans = { free: 0, pro: 0, elite: 0 };\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  (planData || []).forEach((p: any) => {\n    const plan = (p.plan || \"free\").toLowerCase();\n    if (plan in plans) plans[plan as keyof typeof plans]++;\n    else plans.free++;\n  });\n\n  // Active users last 7 and 30 days\n  const now = new Date();\n  const d7 = new Date(now.getTime() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();\n  const d30 = new Date(now.getTime() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();\n\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { count: active7d } = await (supabaseAdmin.from(\"profiles\") as any)\n    .select(\"id\", { count: \"exact\", head: true })\n    .gte(\"last_active_date\", d7.split(\"T\")[0]);\n\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { count: active30d } = await (supabaseAdmin.from(\"profiles\") as any)\n    .select(\"id\", { count: \"exact\", head: true })\n    .gte(\"last_active_date\", d30.split(\"T\")[0]);\n\n  // Signups per week (last 12 weeks)\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { data: signupData } = await (supabaseAdmin.from(\"profiles\") as any)\n    .select(\"created_at\")\n    .gte(\n      \"created_at\",\n      new Date(now.getTime() - 84 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString()\n    )\n    .order(\"created_at\");\n\n  const weeklySignups: { week: string; count: number }[] = [];\n  for (let i = 11; i >= 0; i--) {\n    const weekStart = new Date(\n      now.getTime() - (i + 1) * 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000\n    );\n    const weekEnd = new Date(now.getTime() - i * 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);\n    const label = `${weekStart.getMonth() + 1}/${weekStart.getDate()}`;\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n    const count = (signupData || []).filter((s: any) => {\n      const d = new Date(s.created_at);\n      return d >= weekStart && d < weekEnd;\n    }).length;\n    weeklySignups.push({ week: label, count });\n  }\n\n  // DAU last 30 days from usage table\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { data: usageData } = await (supabaseAdmin.from(\"usage\") as any)\n    .select(\"date, user_id\")\n    .gte(\"date\", d30.split(\"T\")[0]);\n\n  const dauMap = new Map<string, Set<string>>();\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  (usageData || []).forEach((u: any) => {\n    if (!dauMap.has(u.date)) dauMap.set(u.date, new Set());\n    dauMap.get(u.date)!.add(u.user_id);\n  });\n\n  const dailyActive: { date: string; users: number }[] = [];\n  for (let i = 29; i >= 0; i--) {\n    const d = new Date(now.getTime() - i * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);\n    const dateStr = d.toISOString().split(\"T\")[0];\n    dailyActive.push({\n      date: `${d.getMonth() + 1}/${d.getDate()}`,\n      users: dauMap.get(dateStr)?.size || 0,\n    });\n  }\n\n  // Module usage last 30 days\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { data: moduleData } = await (supabaseAdmin.from(\"usage\") as any)\n    .select(\"solves, writes, humanizes, learns\")\n    .gte(\"date\", d30.split(\"T\")[0]);\n\n  const modules = { solves: 0, writes: 0, humanizes: 0, learns: 0 };\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  (moduleData || []).forEach((m: any) => {\n    modules.solves += m.solves || 0;\n    modules.writes += m.writes || 0;\n    modules.humanizes += m.humanizes || 0;\n    modules.learns += m.learns || 0;\n  });\n\n  return {\n    totalUsers: totalUsers || 0,\n    activeOrgs: activeOrgs || 0,\n    active7d: active7d || 0,\n    active30d: active30d || 0,\n    plans,\n    weeklySignups,\n    dailyActive,\n    modules,\n  };\n}\n\n// ÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇ User Management ÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇ\n\nexport async function getAdminUsers(filters: {\n  search?: string;\n  plan?: string;\n  active?: string;\n  page?: number;\n  pageSize?: number;\n  sortBy?: string;\n  sortDir?: string;\n}) {\n  await requireSuperAdmin();\n\n  const page = filters.page || 1;\n  const pageSize = filters.pageSize || 25;\n  const offset = (page - 1) * pageSize;\n\n  // Get all user IDs that ARE in an org\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { data: orgMembers } = await (\n    supabaseAdmin.from(\"org_memberships\") as any\n  ).select(\"user_id\");\n  const orgUserIds = new Set(\n    (orgMembers || []).map((m: { user_id: string }) => m.user_id)\n  );\n\n  // Build query for profiles\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  let query = (supabaseAdmin.from(\"profiles\") as any).select(\n    \"id, full_name, email, plan, created_at, last_active_date, current_streak, avatar_url, badges\",\n    { count: \"exact\" }\n  );\n\n  // Search filter\n  if (filters.search) {\n    query = query.or(\n      `full_name.ilike.%${filters.search}%,email.ilike.%${filters.search}%`\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Plan filter\n  if (filters.plan && filters.plan !== \"all\") {\n    query = query.eq(\"plan\", filters.plan);\n  }\n\n  // Activity filter\n  if (filters.active === \"active\") {\n    const d30 = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)\n      .toISOString()\n      .split(\"T\")[0];\n    query = query.gte(\"last_active_date\", d30);\n  } else if (filters.active === \"inactive\") {\n    const d30 = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)\n      .toISOString()\n      .split(\"T\")[0];\n    query = query.or(`last_active_date.lt.${d30},last_active_date.is.null`);\n  }\n\n  // Sort\n  const sortBy = filters.sortBy || \"created_at\";\n  const ascending = filters.sortDir === \"asc\";\n  query = query.order(sortBy, { ascending });\n\n  // Pagination ÔÇö fetch more to filter out org users\n  query = query.range(0, page * pageSize + pageSize * 3);\n\n  const { data, count, error } = await query;\n  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);\n\n  // Filter out org users client-side\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const individualUsers = (data || []).filter(\n    (u: any) => !orgUserIds.has(u.id)\n  );\n\n  // Manual pagination on filtered results\n  const paged = individualUsers.slice(offset, offset + pageSize);\n\n  // Get total problems solved for these users from student_profiles\n  const userIds = paged.map((u: { id: string }) => u.id);\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { data: studentData } = await (\n    supabaseAdmin.from(\"student_profiles\") as any\n  )\n    .select(\"user_id, total_problems_solved\")\n    .in(\"user_id\", userIds.length > 0 ? userIds : [\"__none__\"]);\n\n  const solvedMap = new Map<string, number>();\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  (studentData || []).forEach((s: any) => {\n    solvedMap.set(s.user_id, s.total_problems_solved || 0);\n  });\n\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const users = paged.map((u: any) => ({\n    id: u.id,\n    full_name: u.full_name,\n    email: u.email,\n    plan: u.plan || \"free\",\n    created_at: u.created_at,\n    last_active_date: u.last_active_date,\n    current_streak: u.current_streak || 0,\n    badges_count: Array.isArray(u.badges) ? u.badges.length : 0,\n    problems_solved: solvedMap.get(u.id) || 0,\n  }));\n\n  return {\n    users,\n    total: count ? count - orgUserIds.size : individualUsers.length,\n    page,\n    pageSize,\n  };\n}\n\n// ÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇ Demo Org Helper getManagerRole(orgId: string): Promise<{ userId: string; role: string } | null> {\n  const supabase = await createClient();\n  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\n  if (!user) return null;\n\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { data: membership } = await (supabase.from(\"org_memberships\") as any)\n    .select(\"role\")\n    .eq(\"user_id\", user.id)\n    .eq(\"org_id\", orgId)\n    .single();\n\n  if (membership) {\n    const elevated = [\"teacher\", \"admin\", \"director\", \"secretary\"];\n    if (!elevated.includes(membership.role)) return null;\n    return { userId: user.id, role: membership.role };\n  }\n\n  // Super_admin bypass: read-only director-level access without membership\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { data: profile } = await (supabase.from(\"profiles\") as any)\n    .select(\"is_super_admin\").eq(\"id\", user.id).single();\n  if (profile?.is_super_admin) return { userId: user.id, role: \"director\" };\n\n  return null;\n}\n\nasync function getClassOrgId(classId: string): Promise<{ orgId: string; teacherId: string | null } | classId)\n    .single();\n  return data ? { orgId: data.org_id, teacherId: data.teacher_id } : null;\n}\n\nasync function getClassStudentIds(classId: string): Promise<string[]> {\n  // (data || []).map((m: { user_id: string }) => m.user_id);\n}\n\n// ÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇ Time Buckets ÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇ\n\nfunction getDefaultRange(): DateRange {\n  const now = new Date();\n  return {\n    startDate: new Date(now.getTime() - 30 * 86400000).toISOString().split(\"T\")[0],\n    endDate: now.toISOString().split(\"T\")[0],\n  };\n}\n\nfunction computeTimeBuckets(startDate: string, endDate: string): { label: string; start: Date; end: Date }[] {\n  const start = new Date(startDate);\n  const end = new Date(endDate);\n  const diffDays = Math.ceil((end.getTime() - start.getTime()) / 86400000);\n\n  if (diffDays <= 7) {\n    // Daily buckets\n    const buckets = [];\n    for (let d = 0; d < diffDays; d++) {\n      const bStart = new Date(start.getTime() + d * 86400000);\n      const bEnd = new Date(start.getTime() + (d + 1) * 86400000);\n      buckets.push({\n        label: `${bStart.getMonth() + 1}/${bStart.getDate()}`,\n        start: bStart,\n        end: bEnd,\n      });\n    }\n    return buckets;\n  } else if (diffDays <= 90) {\n    // Weekly buckets\n    const weeks = Math.ceil(diffDays / 7);\n    const buckets = [];\n    for (let w = 0; w < weeks; w++) {\n      const wStart = new Date(start.getTime() + w * 7 * 86400000);\n      const wEnd = new Date(Math.min(start.getTime() + (w + 1) * 7 * 86400000, end.getTime()));\n      buckets.push({\n        label: `${wStart.getMonth() + 1}/${wStart.getDate()}`,\n        start: wStart,\n        end: wEnd,\n      });\n    }\n    return buckets;\n  } else {\n    // Monthly buckets\n    const buckets = [];\n    const cursor = new Date(start.getFullYear(), start.getMonth(), 1);\n    while (cursor < end) {\n      const mStart = new Date(cursor);\n      cursor.setMonth(cursor.getMonth() + 1);\n      const mEnd = new Date(Math.min(cursor.getTime(), end.getTime()));\n      const monthNames = [\"Jan\", \"Fev\", \"Mar\", \"Abr\", \"Mai\", \"Jun\", \"Jul\", \"Ago\", \"Set\", \"Out\", \"Nov\", \"Dez\"];\n      buckets.push({\n        label: monthNames[mStart.getMonth()],\n        start: mStart,\n        end: mEnd,\n      });\n    }\n    return buckets;\n  }\n}\n\n// ÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇ Teacher Dashboard getTeacherDashboard(classId: string, dateRange?: DateRange) {\n  const classInfo = await getClassOrgId(classId);\n  if (!classInfo) return null;\n\n  const supabase = await createClient();\n  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\n  if (!user) return null;\n\n  let authorized = classInfo.teacherId === user.id;\n  if (!authorized) {\n    const mgr = await getManagerRole(classInfo.orgId);\n    if (mgr && [\"admin\", \"director\", \"secretary\"].includes(mgr.role)) authorized = true;\n  }\n  if (!authorized) return null;\n\n  const studentIds = await getClassStudentIds(classId);\n  if (studentIds.length === 0) {\n    return { empty: true, studentCount: 0 };\n  }\n\n  const { startDate, endDate } = dateRange || getDefaultRange();\n  const now = new Date(endDate + \"T23:59:59Z\");\n  const startISO = new Date(startDate).toISOString();\n  const diffDays = Math.ceil((now.getTime() - new Date(startDate).getTime()) / 86400000);\n  const inactiveThreshold = Math.max(7, Math.round(diffDays * 0.15));\n  const inactiveDate = new Date(now.getTime() - inactiveThreshold * 86400000).toISOString().split(\"T\")[0];\n\n  // Batch fetch all data (filtered by date range)\n  const [profilesRes, challengeRes, usageRes, kmRes, spRes] = await Promise.all([\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n    (supabaseAdmin.from(\"profiles\") as any)\n      .select(\"id, full_name, email, last_active_date, current_streak\")\n      .in(\"id\", studentIds),\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n    (supabaseAdmin.from(\"challenge_log\") as any)\n      .select(\"user_id, topic, subject, created_at\")\n      .in(\"user_id\", studentIds)\n      .gte(\"created_at\", startISO)\n      .lte(\"created_at\", new Date(endDate + \"T23:59:59Z\").toISOString())\n      .order(\"created_at\"),\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n    (supabaseAdmin.from(\"usage\") as any)\n      .select(\"user_id, date, solves, writes, learns\")\n      .in(\"user_id\", studentIds)\n      .gte(\"date\", activeInPeriod = profiles.filter((p: any) =>\n    p.last_active_date && p.last_active_date >= startDate\n  ).length;\n\n  // Accuracy from student_profiles (all-time, since challenge_log doesn't have is_correct)\n   \n  const spMap = new Map<string, { solved: number; correct: number }>();\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  spData.forEach((sp: any) => {\n    spMap.set(sp.id, { solved: sp.total_problems_solved || 0, correct: sp.total_correct || 0 });\n  });\n  const totalSolvedFromSP = spData.reduce((s: number, sp: any) => s + (sp.total_problems_solved || 0), 0);\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const totalCorrectFromSP = spData.reduce((s: number, sp: any) => s + (sp.total_correct || 0), 0);\n\n  // Period-based solved count from challenge_log\n  const totalSolvedInPeriod = challenges.length;\n  // Per-student challenge count in period\n  const studentChallengeCount = new Map<string, number>();\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  challenges.forEach((c: any) => {\n    studentChallengeCount.set(c.user_id, (studentChallengeCount.get(c.user_id) || 0) + 1);\n  });\n\n  const avgSolved = Math.round(totalSolvedInPeriod / studentIds.length);\n  const avgAccuracy = totalSolvedFromSP > 0 ? Math.round((totalCorrectFromSP / totalSolvedFromSP) * 100) : 0;\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const avgStreak = Math.round(profiles.reduce((s: number, p: any) => s + (p.current_streak || 0), 0) / studentIds.length);\n\n  // Evolution with dynamic time buckets\n  const buckets = computeTimeBuckets(startDate, endDate);\n  const evolution = buckets.map((b) => {\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n    const bChallenges = challenges.filter((c: any) => {\n      const d = new Date(c.created_at);\n      return d >= b.start && d < b.end;\n    });\n    const solved = bChallenges.length;\n    return {\n      week: b.label,\n      solved,\n      accuracy: avgAccuracy, // Use all-time accuracy since we can't compute per-bucket\n    };\n  });\n\n  // Accuracy distribution (from student_profiles, per student)\n  const accuracyBuckets = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0];\n  const accuracyLabels = [\"0-30%\", \"30-50%\", \"50-70%\", \"70-90%\", \"90-100%\"];\n  studentIds.forEach((sid: string) => {\n    const sp = spMap.get(sid);\n    const acc = sp && sp.solved > 0 ? (sp.correct / sp.solved) * 100 : 0;\n    if (acc < 30) accuracyBuckets[0]++;\n    else if (acc < 50) accuracyBuckets[1]++;\n    else if (acc < 70) accuracyBuckets[2]++;\n    else if (acc < 90) accuracyBuckets[3]++;\n    else accuracyBuckets[4]++;\n  });\n  const accuracyDist = accuracyLabels.map((label, i) => ({ range: label, count: accuracyBuckets[i] }));\n\n  // Top 5 error topics (based on frequency ÔÇö we can't track correctness per challenge)\n  const topicCounts = new Map<string, number>();\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  challenges.forEach((c: any) => {\n    const key = `${c.subject} ÔÇö ${c.topic}`;\n    topicCounts.set(key, (topicCounts.get(key) || 0) + 1);\n  });\n  // Show topics with lowest avg accuracy from student_profiles\n  // Since we can't track per-topic errors without is_correct, show most-practiced topics\n  const topErrors = Array.from(topicCounts.entries())\n    .map(([topic, total]) => ({ topic, errors: 0, total, errorRate: 0 }))\n    .sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total)\n    .slice(0, 5);\n\n  // Inactive students (relative to period)\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const inactiveStudents = profiles.filter((p: any) => {\n    if (!p.last_active_date) return true;\n    return p.last_active_date < inactiveDate;\n  })\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n    .map((p: any) => ({\n      name: p.full_name || p.email?.split(\"@\")[0] || \"User\",\n      lastActive: p.last_active_date,\n      daysInactive: p.last_active_date\n        ? Math.floor((now.getTime() - new Date(p.last_active_date).getTime()) / 86400000)\n        : 999,\n    }))\n    .sort((a: { daysInactive: number }, b: { daysInactive: number }) => b.daysInactive - a.daysInactive)\n    .slice(0, 10);\n\n  // Module usage\n  const modules = { solves: 0, writes: 0, learns: 0 };\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  usageData.forEach((u: any) => {\n    modules.solves += u.solves || 0;\n    modules.writes += u.writes || 0;\n    modules.learns += u.learns || 0;\n  });\n  const moduleUsage = Object.entries(modules).map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value }));\n\n  // Mastery distribution (current snapshot, not period-dependent)\n  const masteryBuckets = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0];\n  const masteryLabels = [\"Iniciante\", \"B├ísico\", \"Intermedi├írio\", \"Avan├ºado\", \"Expert\"];\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  kmData.forEach((km: any) => {\n    const pct = km.mastery_pct || 0;\n    const level = Math.min(4, Math.floor(pct / 20));\n    masteryBuckets[level]++;\n  });\n  const masteryDist = masteryLabels.map((label, i) => ({ level: label, count: masteryBuckets[i] }));\n\n  return {\n    empty: false,\n    studentCount: studentIds.length,\n    active7d: activeInPeriod,\n    avgSolved,\n    avgAccuracy,\n    avgStreak,\n    weeklyEvolution: evolution,\n    accuracyDist,\n    topErrors,\n    inactiveStudents,\n    moduleUsage,\n    masteryDist,\n  };\n}\n\n// ÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇ Director Dashboard ÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇ\n\nexport async function getDirectorDashboard(orgId: string, dateRange?: DateRange) {\n  const mgr = await getManagerRole(orgId);\n  if (!mgr || ![\"admin\", \"director\", \"secretary\"].includes(mgr.role)) return null;\n\n  const { startDate, endDate } = dateRange || getDefaultRange();\n  const startISO = new Date(startDate).toISOString();\n\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { data: classes } = await (supabaseAdmin.from(\"classes\") as any)\n    .select(\"id, name\").eq(\"org_id\", orgId);\n\n  if (!classes || classes.length === 0) return { empty: true, classCount: 0 };\n\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const classComparison: any[] = [];\n  let totalStudentsAll = 0;\n  let totalSolvedAll = 0;\n  let totalCorrectAll = 0;\n  let totalSolvedFromSP = 0;\n  let activeAll = 0;\n  let totalStreakAll = 0;\n\n  for (const cls of classes) {\n    const sids = await getClassStudentIds(cls.id);\n    if (sids.length === 0) {\n      classComparison.push({ classId: cls.id, className: cls.name, students: 0, active7d: 0, avgSolved: 0, avgAccuracy: 0, adoption: 0 });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const [pRes, challengeRes, spRes] = await Promise.all([\n      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n      (supabaseAdmin.from(\"profiles\") as any)\n        .select(\"id, last_active_date, current_streak\")\n        .in(\"id\", sids),\n      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n      (supabaseAdmin.from(\"challenge_log\") as any)\n        .select(\"user_id\")\n        .in(\"user_id\", sids)\n        .gte(\"created_at\", startISO)\n        .lte(\"created_at\", new Date(endDate + \"T23:59:59Z\").toISOString()),\n      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n      (supabaseAdmin.from(\"student_profiles\") as any)\n        .select(\"id, total_problems_solved, total_correct\")\n        .in(\"id\", sids),\n    ]);\n\n    const profs = pRes.data || [];\n    const chals = challengeRes.data || [];\n    const spRows = spRes.data || [];\n\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n    const classActive = profs.filter((p: any) => p.last_active_date && p.last_active_date >= startDate).length;\n    const classSolved = chals.length;\n    // Accuracy from student_profiles (all-time)\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n    const classTotalSolved = spRows.reduce((s: number, sp: any) => s + (sp.total_problems_solved || 0), 0);\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n    const classTotalCorrect = spRows.reduce((s: number, sp: any) => s + (sp.total_correct || 0), 0);\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n    const classStreak = profs.reduce((s: number, p: any) => s + (p.current_streak || 0), 0);\n\n    classComparison.push({\n      classId: cls.id,\n      className: cls.name,\n      students: sids.length,\n      active7d: classActive,\n      avgSolved: Math.round(classSolved / sids.length),\n      avgAccuracy: classTotalSolved > 0 ? Math.round((classTotalCorrect / classTotalSolved) * 100) : 0,\n      adoption: Math.round((classActive / sids.length) * 100),\n    });\n\n    totalStudentsAll += sids.length;\n    totalSolvedAll += classSolved;\n    totalCorrectAll += classTotalCorrect;\n    activeAll += classActive;\n    totalStreakAll += classStreak;\n    totalSolvedFromSP += classTotalSolved;\n  }\n\n  // Weekly/daily evolution (school-wide)\n  const allStudentIds = (await Promise.all(\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n    classes.map((c: any) => getClassStudentIds(c.id))\n  )).flat();\n\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { data: challengeData } = await (supabaseAdmin.from(\"challenge_log\") as any)\n    .select(\"created_at\")\n    .in(\"user_id\", allStudentIds.length > 0 ? allStudentIds : [\"__none__\"])\n    .gte(\"created_at\", startISO)\n    .lte(\"created_at\", new Date(endDate + \"T23:59:59Z\").toISOString())\n    .order(\"created_at\");\n\n  const buckets = computeTimeBuckets(startDate, endDate);\n  const weeklyEvolution = buckets.map((b) => {\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n    const count = (challengeData || []).filter((c: any) => {\n      const d = new Date(c.created_at);\n      return d >= b.start && d < b.end;\n    }).length;\n    return { week: b.label, solved: count };\n  });\n\n  // Engagement alerts (last 2 weeks within range)\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const engagementAlerts: any[] = [];\n  const endDt = new Date(endDate + \"T23:59:59Z\");\n  const oneWeekBefore = new Date(endDt.getTime() - 7 * 86400000);\n  const twoWeeksBefore = new Date(endDt.getTime() - 14 * 86400000);\n\n  for (const cls of classComparison) {\n    if (cls.students === 0) continue;\n    const sids = await getClassStudentIds(cls.classId);\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n    const { data: tw } = await (supabaseAdmin.from(\"challenge_log\") as any)\n      .select(\"id\")\n      .in(\"user_id\", sids)\n      .gte(\"created_at\", oneWeekBefore.toISOString())\n      .lte(\"created_at\", endDt.toISOString());\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n    const { data: lw } = await (supabaseAdmin.from(\"challenge_log\") as any)\n      .select(\"id\")\n      .in(\"user_id\", sids)\n      .gte(\"created_at\", twoWeeksBefore.toISOString())\n      .lt(\"created_at\", oneWeekBefore.toISOString());\n\n    const twCount = tw?.length || 0;\n    const lwCount = lw?.length || 0;\n    if (lwCount > 0 && twCount < lwCount * 0.7) {\n      engagementAlerts.push({\n        className: cls.className,\n        thisWeek: twCount,\n        lastWeek: lwCount,\n        dropPct: Math.round(((lwCount - Math.round((totalCorrectAll / totalSolvedFromSP) * 100) : 0,\n    active7d: activeAll,\n    avgStreak: totalStudentsAll > 0 ? Math.round(totalStreakAll / totalStudentsAll) : 0,\n    adoption: totalStudentsAll > 0 ? Math.round((activeAll / totalStudentsAll) * 100) : 0,\n    classComparison,\n    weeklyEvolution,\n    engagementAlerts,\n  };\n}\n\n// ÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇ Secretary Dashboard ÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇ\n\nexport async function getSecretaryDashboard(orgId: string, dateRange?: DateRange) {\n  const mgr = await getManagerRole(orgId);\n  if (!mgr || ![\"admin\", \"secretary\"].includes(mgr.role)) return null;\n\n  const { as any).rpc(\"get_org_subtree\", { root_id: orgId });\n  const childIds = (subtree || [])\n    .filter((n: { depth: number }) => n.depth > 0)\n    .map((n: { org_id: string }) => n.org_id);\n\n  if (childIds.length === 0) return { empty: true, schoolCount: 0 };\n\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const { data: childOrgs } = await (supabaseAdmin.from(\"organizations\") as any)\n    .select(\"id, name, type\").in(\"id\", childIds);\n\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n  const schoolComparison: any)\n      .select(\"user_id\").eq(\"org_id\", org.id).eq(\"role\", \"student\");\n    const sids = (members || []).map((m: { user_id: string }) => m.user_id);\n\n    if (sids.length === 0) {\n      schoolComparison.push({ orgId: org.id, orgName: org.name, orgType: org.type, students: 0, active7d: 0, avgSolved: 0, avgAccuracy: 0, adoption: 0 });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const [pRes, .in(\"user_id\", sids)\n        .gte(\"created_at\", new Date(startDate).toISOString())\n        .lte(\"created_at\", new Date((dateRange || getDefaultRange()).endDate + \"T23:59:59Z\").toISOString()),\n      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n      (supabaseAdmin.from(\"student_profiles\") as any)\n        .select(\"id, total_problems_solved, total_correct\")\n        .in(\"id\", sids),\n    ]);\n\n    const profs = pRes.data || [];\n    const chals = challengeRes.data || [];\n    const spRows = spRes.data || [];\n\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n    const active = profs.filter((p: any) => p.last_active_date && p.last_active_date >= startDate).length;\n    const solved = chals.length;\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n    const spTotalSolved = spRows.reduce((s: number, sp: any) => s + (sp.total_problems_solved || 0), 0);\n    // orgId: org.id,\n      orgName: org.name,\n      orgType: org.type,\n      students: sids.length,\n      active7d: active,\n      avgSolved: Math.round(solved / sids.length),\n      avgAccuracy: spTotalSolved > 0 ? Math.round((spTotalCorrect / spTotalSolved) * 100) : 0,\n      adoption: Math.round((active / sids.length) * 100),\n    });\n  }\n\n  schoolComparison.sort((a, b) => b.avgSolved - a.avgSolved);\n\n  return {\n    empty: false,\n    schoolCount: childOrgs?.length || 0,\n    schoolComparison,\n  };\n}\n","usedDeprecatedRules":[]},{"filePath":"C:\\axiom\\src\\lib\\actions\\invite-codes.ts","messages":[],"suppressedMessages":[{"ruleId":"@typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any","severity":1,"message":"Unexpected any. all organizations the current user is a member of,\n * PLUS child orgs visible through hierarchy for elevated roles.\n */\nexport async function getMyOrganizations() {\n  const supabase = await createClient();\n  const {\n    data: { user },\n  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\n  if (!user) return [];\n\n  // Use supabaseAdmin ÔÇö memberships created via redeemInviteCode (supabaseAdmin)\n .select(\"status\")\n    .eq(\"id\", orgId)\n    .single();\n  if (orgCheck?.status !== \"active\") {\n    if (!isSuperAdminUser) return null;\n  }\n\n  // Fetch org, members, classes in parallel\n interface StudentReportTopic {\r\n  name: string;\r\n  level: number;\r\n  mastery_percent: number;\r\n  correct_count: number;\r\n  incorrect_count: number;\r\n  interactions: number;\r\n  last_interaction: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface StudentReportSubject {\r\n  name: string;\r\n  topics: StudentReportTopic[];\r\n  avg_mastery: number;\r\n  total_topics: number;\r\n  topics_mastered: number;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface StudentReportBadge {\r\n  name: string;\r\n  icon: string;\r\n  unlocked_at: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface StudentReport {\r\n  student: {\r\n    name: string;\r\n    email: string;\r\n    avatar_url: string | null;\r\n    plan: string;\r\n    school_year: string | null;\r\n    learning_goal: string | null;\r\n  };\r\n  class: {\r\n    name: string;\r\n    org_name: string;\r\n  };\r\n  stats: {\r\n    total_solved: number;\r\n    total_correct: number;\r\n    accuracy_percent: number;\r\n    current_streak: number;\r\n    badges_count: number;\r\n    days_active: number;\r\n    member_since: string;\r\n  };\r\n  subjects: StudentReportSubject[];\r\n  activity: {\r\n    days_active_30d: number;\r\n    exercises_30d: number;\r\n    trend: \"improving\" | \"stable\" | \"declining\";\r\n  };\r\n  usage: {\r\n    solves: number;\r\n    writes: number;\r\n    humanizes: number;\r\n    learns: number;\r\n  };\r\n  badges: StudentReportBadge[];\r\n  strengths: string[];\r\n  weaknesses: string[];\r\n  generated_at: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\n// ÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇ Main Action ÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇÔöÇ\r\n\r\nexport async function getStudentReport(\r\n  studentId: string,\r\n  classId: string\r\n): Promise<StudentReport | null> {\r\n  const supabase = await createClient();\r\n  const {\r\n    data: { user },\r\n  } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\r\n  if (!user) return null;\r\n\r\n  // Check super_admin for RLS bypass (same pattern as rankings.ts)\r\n  let isSuperAdminUser = false;\r\n  {\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n    const { data: prof } = await (supabase.from(\"profiles\") as any)\r\n      .select(\"is_super_admin\").eq(\"id\", user.id).single();\r\n    isSuperAdminUser = prof?.is_super_admin === true;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n  const db: any = isSuperAdminUser ? supabaseAdmin : supabase;\r\n\r\n  // 1. Get class ÔåÆ org_id\r\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n  const { data: cls } = await (db.from(\"classes\") as any)\r\n    .select(\"id, name, org_id, teacher_id\")\r\n    .eq(\"id\", classId)\r\n    .single();\r\n  if (!cls) return null;\r\n\r\n  // 2. Auth: super_admin bypasses, otherwise check elevated role\r\n  if (!isSuperAdminUser) {\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n    const { data: membership } = await (db.from(\"org_memberships\") as any)\r\n      .select(\"role\")\r\n      .eq(\"user_id\", user.id)\r\n      .eq(\"org_id\", cls.org_id)\r\n      .single();\r\n    const elevated = [\"teacher\", \"admin\", \"director\", \"secretary\"];\r\n    const isTeacher = cls.teacher_id === user.id;\r\n    if (!isTeacher && (!membership || !elevated.includes(membership.role))) {\r\n      return null;\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // 3. Verify student is in this class\r\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n  const { data: membership } = await (db.from(\"class_memberships\") as any)\r\n    .select(\"joined_at\")\r\n    .eq(\"class_id\", classId)\r\n    .eq(\"user_id\", studentId)\r\n    .single();\r\n  if (!membership) return null;\r\n\r\n  // 4. Parallel fetch all data\r\n  const thirtyDaysAgo = new Date();\r\n  thirtyDaysAgo.setDate(thirtyDaysAgo.getDate() - 30);\r\n  const thirtyDaysISO = thirtyDaysAgo.toISOString();\r\n\r\n  const [\r\n    profileRes,\r\n    spRes,\r\n    orgRes,\r\n    kmRes,\r\n    challengeRes,\r\n    challenge30dRes,\r\n    usageRes,\r\n    badgeRes,\r\n    catalogRes,\r\n  ] = await Promise.all([\r\n    // Profile\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n    (db.from(\"profiles\") as any)\r\n      .select(\"full_name, email, avatar_url, plan, current_streak, created_at\")\r\n      .eq(\"id\", studentId)\r\n      .single(),\r\n    // Student profile\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n    (db.from(\"student_profiles\") as any)\r\n      .select(\"total_problems_solved, total_correct, school_year, learning_goal\")\r\n      .eq(\"id\", studentId)\r\n      .single(),\r\n    // Organization name\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n    (db.from(\"organizations\") as any)\r\n      .select(\"name\")\r\n      .eq(\"id\", cls.org_id)\r\n      .single(),\r\n    // Knowledge map ÔÇö all entries (use select * to handle optional MBLID columns)\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n    (db.from(\"knowledge_map\") as any)\r\n      .select(\"*\")\r\n      .eq(\"user_id\", studentId)\r\n      .order(\"subject\")\r\n      .order(\"mastery_score\", { ascending: false }),\r\n    // Challenge log ÔÇö all time (for days_active count)\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n    (db.from(\"challenge_log\") as any)\r\n      .select(\"created_at\")\r\n      .eq(\"user_id\", studentId),\r\n    // Challenge log ÔÇö last 30 days\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n    (db.from(\"challenge_log\") as any)\r\n      .select(\"created_at\")\r\n      .eq(\"user_id\", studentId)\r\n      .gte(\"created_at\", thirtyDaysISO),\r\n    // Usage\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n    (db.from(\"usage\") as any)\r\n      .select(\"solves, writes, humanizes, learns\")\r\n      .eq(\"user_id\", studentId),\r\n    // Badges\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n    (db.from(\"user_badges\") as any)\r\n      .select(\"badge_id, unlocked_at\")\r\n      .eq(\"user_id\", studentId)\r\n      .order(\"unlocked_at\", { ascending: false }),\r\n    // Badge catalog\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n    (db.from(\"badges_catalog\") as any)\r\n      .select(\"id, icon, name_key\")\r\n      .order(\"sort_order\"),\r\n  ]);\r\n\r\n  const profile = profileRes.data;\r\n  const sp = spRes.data;\r\n  const org = orgRes.data;\r\n  if (!profile) return null;\r\n\r\n  // 5. Process knowledge map ÔåÆ subjects\r\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n  const kmRows: any[] = kmRes.data || [];\r\n  const subjectMap = new Map<string, StudentReportTopic[]>();\r\n  for (const row of kmRows) {\r\n    const topics = subjectMap.get(row.subject) || [];\r\n    topics.push({\r\n      name: row.topic,\r\n      level: row.level || 1,\r\n      mastery_percent: Math.round((row.mastery_score || 0) * 100),\r\n      correct_count: row.correct_count || 0,\r\n      incorrect_count: row.incorrect_count || 0,\r\n      interactions: row.interactions_count || 0,\r\n      last_interaction: row.last_interaction_at || \"\",\r\n    });\r\n    subjectMap.set(row.subject, topics);\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  const subjects: StudentReportSubject[] = [];\r\n  for (const [name, topics] of subjectMap.entries()) {\r\n    const avgMastery =\r\n      topics.length > 0\r\n        ? Math.round(topics.reduce((s, t) => s + t.mastery_percent, 0) / topics.length)\r\n        : 0;\r\n    subjects.push({\r\n      name,\r\n      topics,\r\n      avg_mastery: avgMastery,\r\n      total_topics: topics.length,\r\n      topics_mastered: topics.filter((t) => t.level >= 4).length,\r\n    });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // 6. Days active (unique dates in challenge_log)\r\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n  const allChallenges: any[] = challengeRes.data || [];\r\n  const uniqueDays = new Set(\r\n    allChallenges.map((c) => new Date(c.created_at).toISOString().split(\"T\")[0])\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  // 7. Activity ÔÇö last 30 days\r\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n  const challenges30d: any[] = challenge30dRes.data || [];\r\n  const uniqueDays30d = new Set(\r\n    challenges30d.map((c) => new Date(c.created_at).toISOString().split(\"T\")[0])\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  // Trend: compare last 15 days vs previous 15 days\r\n  const fifteenDaysAgo = new Date();\r\n  fifteenDaysAgo.setDate(fifteenDaysAgo.getDate() - 15);\r\n  const recentHalf = challenges30d.filter(\r\n    (c) => new Date(c.created_at) >= fifteenDaysAgo\r\n  ).length;\r\n  const olderHalf = challenges30d.length - recentHalf;\r\n  let trend: \"improving\" | \"stable\" | \"declining\" = \"stable\";\r\n  if (olderHalf > 0) {\r\n    const ratio = recentHalf / olderHalf;\r\n    if (ratio > 1.25) trend = \"improving\";\r\n    else if (ratio < 0.75) trend = \"declining\";\r\n  } else if (recentHalf > 0) {\r\n    trend = \"improving\";\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // 8. Usage totals\r\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n  const usageRows: any[] = usageRes.data || [];\r\n  const usage = { solves: 0, writes: 0, humanizes: 0, learns: 0 };\r\n  for (const u of usageRows) {\r\n    usage.solves += u.solves || 0;\r\n    usage.writes += u.writes || 0;\r\n    usage.humanizes += u.humanizes || 0;\r\n    usage.learns += u.learns || 0;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // 9. Badges\r\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\r\n  const catalogMap = new Map<string, { icon: string; name_key: string }>();\r\n  for (const c of (catalogRes.data || []) as { id: string; icon: string; name_key: string }[]) {\r\n    catalogMap.set(c.id, { icon: c.icon, name_key: c.name_key });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Resolve name_key ÔåÆ human-readable display name\r\n  // DB stores name_key like \"Badges.firstSolve.name\" ÔÇö we strip prefix+suffix\r\n  const badgeDisplayNames: Record<string, string> = {\r\n    // Mapped from badges_catalog name_key (after stripping \"Badges.\" and \".name\")\r\n    firstSolve: \"First Solve\",\r\n    solves10: \"10 Problems\",\r\n    solves50: \"50 Problems\",\r\n    solves100: \"100 Problems\",\r\n    firstCorrect: \"First Correct\",\r\n    streak10Correct: \"10 Correct Streak\",\r\n    streak3Day: \"3-Day Streak\",\r\n    streak7Day: \"7-Day Streak\",\r\n    streak30Day: \"30-Day Streak\",\r\n    firstMastery: \"Topic Mastered\",\r\n    mastery5Topics: \"5 Topics Mastered\",\r\n    multiSubject: \"Multi Subject\",\r\n  };\r\n  function humanizeBadgeName(nameKey: string): string {\r\n    // Strip \"Badges.\" prefix and \".name\"/\".desc\" suffix\r\n    const cleanKey = nameKey\r\n      .replace(/^Badges\\./, \"\")\r\n      .replace(/\\.(name|desc)$/, \"\");\r\n    if (badgeDisplayNames[cleanKey]) return badgeDisplayNames[cleanKey];\r\n    // Fallback: \"firstSolve\" ÔåÆ \"First Solve\", \"some_key\" ÔåÆ \"Some Key\"\r\n    return cleanKey\r\n      .replace(/([A-Z])/g, \" $1\")\r\n      .replace(/_/g, \" \")\r\n      .replace(/^\\s+/, \"\")\r\n      .replace(/\\b\\w/g, (c) => c.toUpperCase());\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  const badges: StudentReportBadge[] = (\r\n    (badgeRes.data || []) as { badge_id: string; unlocked_at: string }[]\r\n  ).map((b) => {\r\n    const cat = catalogMap.get(b.badge_id);\r\n    return {\r\n      name: humanizeBadgeName(cat?.name_key || b.badge_id),\r\n      icon: cat?.icon || \"\",\r\n      unlocked_at: b.unlocked_at,\r\n    };\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  // 10.
